## Dedication

->*Seek the Channel of your Soul.*<- <note>original: "Quare Tu Animæ Canalem"; This is a maxim from the Chaldean Oracles, which were ancient mystical texts attributed to Zoroaster. It encourages the seeker to find the divine path or "conduit" within their own soul.</note>

I have a better *Confidence* in your *Opinion* of *me*, than to feel I must tell you, *I love you*: and for my present *Boldness*, you must thank *yourself*, for you taught me this *Familiarity*. I here trouble you with a short *Discourse* <note>A formal written treatment or essay on a specific subject.</note>, the *scraps* <note>original: "Brokage"; literally the business of a broker, but here used metaphorically to mean the gathered fragments or secondhand leftovers of his previous work.</note> and weak *Remembrances* of my former, and more complete *studies*; It is no *labored Work* <note>original: "labour'd Peece"; a formal, highly polished academic or literary composition.</note>, and indeed no fit *Gift*, but I beg your *Acceptance* as if it were a *Warning* <note>original: "Caveat"; a cautionary note or legal warning.</note>, so that you may see what unprofitable *Friendships* <note>original: "Affections"; in this context, the social and emotional bonds the recipient has "invested" in by befriending the author.</note> you have *Purchased*. ## To the Reader.

Princes and Governors, and in that a stately **Lady** <note>original: "Donna"; an Italian title for a woman of high rank.</note>, who like some **Queen regent** <note>A queen ruling in her own right or on behalf of another.</note>, commanded the rest of the Company. Poor **Truth**, she stood still while this *pompous Squadron* passed by, but when the *Chariot* came over against her, the **Lady Lie** who was there seated, took notice of her, and causing her *Pageants* <note>Elaborate floats or decorative stages used in public processions.</note> to stay, commanded her to come nearer. Here she was scornfully examined: where did she come from? where would she go? and what was her business? To these Questions she answered as the Custom of **Truth** is, very simply and plainly: Whereupon the **Lady Lie** commands her to wait upon her, and that in the *Rear*, and *Tail* of all her *Troop*, for that was the known place of **Truth**. Thanks then not to the stars, but to the *arrangements* <note>original: "Configurations"</note> of the **Dice**! they have acquainted me with my future fortunes, and what advancement <note>original: "præferment"</note> my **Book** is like to attain to. I am for my part contented, though the *confide-* <note>The text cuts off here. The catchword "ration" at the bottom of the page suggests the next page begins with the completion of a word like "consideration" or "confidentially."</note>

<summary>The author completes a parable where Truth is forced to follow at the very back of Lady Lie's procession, symbolizing how honesty is often marginalized by falsehood. He uses this story to humbly predict that his own book will occupy a lowly station in the world.</summary>

## To the Reader.

Man, planted in him a *spirit* of that *Capacity* <note>The inherent ability of the human mind to grasp or contain knowledge; its "volume" for understanding.</note>, that he might *know All*, adding thereto a *most* fervent *Desire* to know, lest that *Capacity* should be *useless*. This *Truth* is evident in the *Posterity of Man* <note>The descendants of humanity; i.e., every generation of children.</note>: For little *Children* before ever they can speak, will stare upon any thing, that is *strange* to them; they will cry, and are restless till they get it into their hands, that they may feel it, and look upon it, that is to say, that they may *know* what it is in some *Degree*, and according to their *Capacity*. Now some ignorant *Nurse* <note>A nanny or childcare provider.</note> will think they do all this, out of a desire to play with what they see, but they themselves tell us the *Contrary*; For when they are past *Infants*, and begin to make use of *Language*, if any *New* thing appears, they will not desire to *play* with it, but they will *ask* you, *what it is?* for they desire to *know*; and this is plain out of their *Actions*; for if you put any *Rattle* <note>A common toy for a baby.</note> into their hands,

## To the Reader.

they will *view* it, and *study* it for some short Time, and when they can know *no more*, then they will *play* with it. It is well known, that if you hold a *candle* near to a *little Child*, he will (if you prevent him not) <note>If you do not stop or hinder him.</note> put his *finger* into the *flame*, for he desires to *know* what it is, that shines so bright; but there is something more than all this, for even these *Infants* desire to *improve* their *Knowledge*. Thus when they look upon any thing, if the *sight* informs them not *sufficiently*, they will, if they can, get it into their *hands*, that they may *feel* it: but if the *touch* also does not *satisfy*, they will *put* it into their *Mouths* to *taste* it, as if they would examine Things by *more* senses than *one*. Now this *Desire* to know is born with them, and it is the *Best*, and most *mysterious* part of their *Nature*. It is to be observed, that when *men* come to their full Age, and are *serious* in their *Disquisitions* <note>Formal, systematic investigations or scholarly inquiries.</note>, they are ashamed to *Err* <note>To be mistaken or fall into falsehood; the author suggests that as adults, we fear being wrong, whereas children are driven purely by the search for truth.</note>,

> Therefore it clearly appears in this structure of the World, which we see, that some Truth triumphs; which so often moves, stirs up, entangles, and unfolds our reason; so often it provokes our restless and sleepless mind in marvelous ways—not by chance or by accidents from elsewhere, but by its own inherent and original charms of Nature. Since all these things are not done in vain, it must follow that at some suitable time, we shall finally embrace the Truth of those things that exist. <note>original: "Ergo liquidò apparet in hac Mundi structurâ, quam cernimus, aliquam triumphare Veritatem ; quæ toties rationem nostram commovet, agitat, implicat, explicat ; toties inquietam, toties insomnem miris modis sollicitat, non fortuitis, aut aliunde adventitiis, sed suis & propriis, & originariis Naturæ Illicibus ; quæ omnia cum non fiunt frustra, utiq; contingit, ut Veritatem Eorum quæ sunt, aliquo tandem opportuno tempore amplexemur."</note>

often stirs up, puzzles, and helps our *Reason*; so often solicits her when she is restless, so often when she is watchful, and this by strange means, not casual and adventitious, but by genuine provocations and pleasures of nature; All which *Motions* being not to no purpose, it falls out at last that in some good time we attain to the

But because I would not have you build your *Philosophie* on *Coralls* and *whistles* <note>In the 17th century, corals and whistles were common toys or teething objects given to infants; the author uses them here as a metaphor for superficial or trivial intellectual pursuits.</note>, which are the *Objects* of little Children, of whom we have spoken formerly, I will speak somewhat of those *Elements*, in whose Contemplation a *Man* ought to employ himself, and this *Discourse* may serve as a *Preface* to our whole *Philosophie*.

# To the Reader.

We know the *Sun* does not only dispense heat, but some other secret *Influx* <term>Influx: A flowing in of celestial or spiritual power into the material world.</term>; so did *God* also in the *Creation*, and from him the *Sun* and all the *stars* received what they have, for *God* himself is a *supernatural Sun*, or *fire*, according to that *Oracle* of *Zoroaster* <note>Referring to the *Chaldean Oracles*, a collection of mystical verses once attributed to the ancient Persian prophet Zoroaster and highly regarded by alchemists.</note>,

> *The Maker, who working through himself fashioned the World, was another certain Mass of fire.* <note>original: "Factor, Qui per fe operans fabrefecit Mundum, Quadam ignis Moles erat altera."</note>

He did therefore hatch the *Matter*, and bring out the *secret Essences*, as a *Chick* is brought out of the *shell*, whence that other *Position* <note>A formal statement or philosophical thesis.</note> of the same *Zoroaster*,

> *That all things have been brought forth under one Fire.* <note>original: "Omnia fub uno Igne genita effe."</note>

Neither did he only generate them then, but he also *preserves* them now, with a perpetual *Efflux* <term>Efflux: A continuous outward flow of energy or spirit from a divine source.</term> of heat and *spirit*; Hence he is styled in the *Oracles*,

> *Father of both Men and Gods, abundantly animating Fire, Light, Ether, and the Worlds.* <note>original: "Pater Hominumq, Deûmq; Affatim animans Ignem, Lucem, Æthera, Mundos."</note>

# To the Reader.

This is notice enough:
And now Reader, I must tell you, I have met with some *recent attacks* on my two former *Discourses*, but *truth is its own proof*, and I am so far from being overcome, that I am nowhere even understood. When I first looked at the *Libel* <term>Libel: In this period, a "libel" referred to any defamatory publication or pamphlet intended to damage someone's reputation.</term>, and its *Address* to *Philalethes* <note>The pseudonym of the author, Thomas Vaughan, meaning "Lover of Truth."</note>, I judged the Author to be serious, and that his design was not to abuse me, but to inform himself. This conceit quickly vanished; for perusing his first part, his ears shot out of his skin, and presented him as a perfect *Ass* <note>A reference to the fable of the ass in the lion's skin, or perhaps King Midas, used here to suggest the critic's stupidity was exposed despite his disguise.</note>. His observations are one continued *croaking* <note>original: "Κώαξ" (Koax), the onomatopoeic sound of frogs in Aristophanes' play *The Frogs*, implying the critic's words are mindless noise.</note>, and the *Oyster-women* <note>Oyster-sellers in 17th-century London were notorious for their loud, crude, and abusive street language.</note> read the same *Philosophy* every day. It is a *scurrilous*, senseless piece, and as he well styles himself, a *Chip of a Block-head*.

His qualities indeed are excellent abroad, but they are equal at home: his malice is equal to his ignorance. I laughed to see the fool's disease: a *flow of gall* <note>original: "flux of Gale," likely a pun or misspelling of "gall" (bile), which in the medical theory of the four humors was thought to cause bitterness, anger, and resentment.</note>, which made him still at—

<meta>This page continues the author's satirical rebuttal to his critic. The previous page ended mid-sentence, describing the critic's intellectual and physical failings. # To the Reader.

...the jaws, while another held the printing press for him like *Porphyry’s* basin at *Aristotle’s* well <note>A mocking reference to the Neoplatonist philosopher Porphyry and his relationship to Aristotle’s teachings. Vaughan implies the critic is merely a shallow vessel attempting to catch the "water" (wisdom) from a well he does not understand.</note>. There is something in him unnatural: his excrement runs the wrong way, for his mouth defecates, and he is so far from being a human being that he is a disgrace even to the beasts. These are his qualities; and as for his person, I turn him over to the *dog-whippers* <term>Dog-whippers: Minor church or town officials tasked with removing unruly dogs from public places; Vaughan suggests the critic is a nuisance that needs to be physically driven out.</term>, so that he may be well lashed *from behind* <note>original: "a posteriori." Vaughan puns on the logical term for reasoning from effects to causes, using it here to mean a physical whipping on the critic’s backside.</note>, and bear the *errors* <note>original: "Errata," typically the list of printing mistakes found at the beginning of a book.</note> of his front imprinted on his rear. I cannot yet find a more fitting punishment: for since his *head* could learn nothing but nonsense, as a logical consequence of his anatomy, his *tail* should be taught some sense.

This is all for now; and for my present *discourse*, I wish it the common fate of *truth* and *honesty*: to deserve well, but to be spoken of poorly. As for *applause*, I do not fish in the air to catch it. It is a kind of *popularity* which makes me scorn

<meta>catchword: it,</meta>

<summary>In this conclusion to the preface, Vaughan delivers a biting, scatological critique of his opponent's intellect and character before dismissing the need for public approval. He aligns his work with the "common fortune of Truth," which is to be misunderstood or slandered by the masses.</summary>

# To the Reader.

...it <note>The sentence concludes from the previous page: "It is a kind of praise to lack it [public applause]."</note>, for I defy the *noise* of the *mob* <note>original: "Rout," referring to the disorderly or uneducated masses.</note>, because they do not *observe* the *Truth*, but only the *success* of it <note>Vaughan suggests that the common public judges ideas based on their popularity or outcome rather than their inherent truth.</note>. I therefore commit this *piece* to the *world*, without any *protection* other than its *own worth*, and the *estimation* <term>Æstimat: Valuation or assessment of quality.</term> of that *soul* that *understands* it. As for the *rest*, just as I cannot *force* them, so I will not *beg* for their *approval* <note>original: "Approbation"</note>. 

<margin>Subtle maneuvering</margin>
I would not be great through *impositions* <term>Imposts: Taxes or forced duties; here, Vaughan means he will not demand unearned status from others.</term>, nor rich through *charity petitions* <term>Briefs: Official letters or licenses allowing a person to collect money for a cause; Vaughan is asserting that he does not seek public "handouts" or unmerited support for his ideas.</term>. They may be whatever they wish, and I shall be what I am.

->Eugenius Philalethes<- <note>The pseudonym of Thomas Vaughan, meaning "well-born lover of truth."</note>

## Or,

### The Heaven of the Earth, etc. <note>original: "Cœlum Terræ"</note>

That I should profess *Magic* in this discourse, and justify the practitioners of it as well, is considered *Impiety* by many, but *Religion* to *me*. It is a matter of conscience I have learned from *Authors* greater than myself, and from *Scriptures* greater than both. *Magic* is nothing else but the *Wisdom* of the Creator revealed and planted within the Created world. —whose knowledge and devotion brought them from the *East* to *Jerusalem* <note>A reference to the Biblical Magi, whom Vaughan considers the primary examples of true magicians</note>. Secondly, that this *Art* <note>Magic, as Vaughan defines it: the study of divine and natural mysteries</note> should suffer just as *Religion* does, and for the very same *reason*. The main *motives* <note>reasons or triggers</note> which have caused the present *rents* <note>schisms or tears</note> and *divisions* of the *Church* are the *ceremonies* and *types* <note>symbolic representations or ritual patterns</note> used within it. For without *controversy*, the *Apostles* instituted and left behind them certain *elements* or *signs*, such as *water*, *oil*, *salt*, and *lights*, by which they signified to us some great and reverend *mysteries*. But our *Reformers*, mistaking these things for superstitious, turned them all out of doors <note>completely discarded them</note>.

But truly, it was ill done: for if the *shadow* of *Saint Peter* healed the sick, shall not these *shadowes* <note>symbolic representations</note> of *Christ* do much more? The *Papist* <note>a 17th-century, often derogatory, term for Roman Catholics</note>, on the contrary, not knowing the true significance of these *types*, placed a certain *inherent holiness* in them, and so fell into a very dangerous *idolatry*. I omit many *things* which he invented of his own, such as *images*, *holy lambs* <note>likely referring to the Agnus Dei, wax medallions blessed by the Pope</note>, and *relics*, adding these *dead bones* to the *primitive* and *beautiful body* of the *Church*.

Now, to draw the *parallel*: the *Magicians* also instituted certain *signs* as the *Key* <note>original Latin: "Clavis"</note> to their *Art*, and these were the same as those mentioned before—namely *water*, *oil*, *salt*, and *light*. By these, they tacitly <note>silently or secretly</note> discovered to us their *Three Principles* <note>The alchemical "Tria Prima": Salt, Sulfur, and Mercury, which were thought to compose all material things</note> and the *Light of Nature*, which fills and

<meta>This page continues Vaughan’s critique of how true, philosophical "Magic" has been degraded into superstition. ### The Antiquity of Magic.

...actuates all things. The common *man*, reading through their books but not understanding their true meaning, took *candles*, ordinary *water*, *oil*, and *salt*, and began to consecrate and exorcise them to create his own damnable and devilish *magic*. The *magicians* had a *maxim* <note>a fundamental principle or rule</note> amongst themselves: *No word has power in magic unless it is first formed by the voice of God* <note>original Latin: "Quod nulla vox operatur in Magia, nisi prius Dei voce formetur"</note>. Hence, in their books, there was frequent mention made of the *Word* <note>original: "Verbum"</note> and the *Speech* <note>original: "Sermo"</note>. The common *man*, interpreting this according to his own fancy, invented his *charms* and *incantations* <note>original: "Vocabula"; specific words or names used in spells</note>, by which he promised to perform wonders.

The *magicians* in their writings spoke much of *triangles* and *circles*, through which they hinted to us their more secret *triplicity* <note>the threefold nature of reality—often referring to the trinity of salt, sulfur, and mercury, or body, soul, and spirit</note>, along with the *rotation* <note>the cyclic process or revolution</note> of *nature* from the beginning of her *week* to her *Sabbath* <note>original: "Sabaoth"; referring to the completion or divine rest of a natural cycle</note>. By this *circle* or *rotation*, they also claimed that *spirits* might be bound, meaning that the *soul* might be *united* to the *body*. Immediately upon hearing this, the *common man* imagined his own *triangles* and *characters* <note>written symbols or sigils used in occultism</note>, with many strange *diagrams* <note>literally "cobwebs" in the original, implying intricate, confusing, or flimsy designs</note> or *figures*, and a *circle* in which to *conjure*. But not knowing what *spirit* it was that the *magicians* were binding, he struggled and studied instead to bind the *devil*.

Now, if you ask me who these *magicians* were? I must tell you, they were

Kings, they were *Priests*, they were *Prophets*:
*Men* that were acquainted with the *Substantial, Spiritual Mysteries* of *Religion*, and did deal, or dispense the outward *Typical part* <note>the symbolic or ceremonial exterior of faith</note> of it to the *People*. Here then we may see how *Magic* came to be out of *Request*; For the *Lawyers* and *Common Divines* <note>clergymen who lack deeper esoteric or mystical knowledge</note>, who knew not these *Secrets*, perusing the *Ceremonial, Superstitious Trash* of some *Scribblers*, who pretended to *Magic*, prescribed against the *Art* itself as *Impious*, and *Antichristian*, so that it was a *Capital sin* to profess it, and the *Punishment* no less than *Death*. In the *Interim* those few who were *Masters* of the *Science*, observing the first *Monitories* <note>warnings or instructions</note> of it, buried all in a deep *Silence*.

The *Leaders* of this brave *Body*, were *Cornelius Agrippa*, *Libanius Gallus* <note>a teacher of Trithemius</note>, the *Philosopher*, *Johannes Trithemius*, *Georgius Venetus* <note>Francesco Giorgio, a Venetian monk and author of "On the Harmony of the World"</note>, *Johannes Reuchlin*, called in the *Greek Capnion* <note>original Greek: "Kapnion," meaning "little smoke," a Greek translation of his German name Reuchlin</note>, with several others in their several *Days*. vastly with much more content and advantage, I think it not enough to have discovered the *abuses* and *misfortunes* this *science* has suffered, unless I endeavor at the same time to demonstrate the *antiquity* of it. # Adamic Magic, or

First, we must consider the *Curse* itself, and next the *extent* <note>original: *Latitude*; the scope or reach of the curse across the physical world.</note> of it. To reveal the *Nature* of the *Curse*, and what it truly was, you must understand that *Good* *essentially* is *Light*, and *Evil* is *Darkness*. *Evil* is properly a *Corruption* that immediately takes place upon the *Removal* of that which is *Good*. Thus, *God* having removed his *Candlestick* and *Light* <note>A metaphor for the divine spark or vital influence that sustains the created world.</note> from the *Elements*, the *Darkness* and *Cold* of *Matter* <note>In the philosophy of this period, "Matter" was seen as naturally cold and inert, requiring the heat of the Divine Spirit to become vital and productive.</note> immediately prevailed; so it was that the *Earth* was closer to her first *Deformity* <note>The chaotic, unformed state of the earth before the Creation described in Genesis.</note>, and by consequence less fruitful and *Vital*. *Heaven* and *Hell*—that is, *Light* and *Darkness*—are the *two Extremes* which bring *Good* and *Evil* to their ultimate fulfillment.

But there are some *lesser blessings* <note>original: *mean Blessings*; intermediate or secondary gifts compared to eternal salvation.</note> which are only *in order* <note>original: *in ordine*; in the proper sequence or arrangement.</note> to, or preparing the way for, *Heaven*, which is their *final perfection*; and such were these *Blessings* which *God* recalled upon the *Transgression* of the first *Man*. Again, there are some *Evils* which are merely *Degrees* leading to their *final Extremity*, or *Hell*; and such was this *Curse* or *Evil* which followed the *Transgression*. 

Thus our *Savior*, using these *Concepts* of *Blessed* and *Cursed*, categorizes the *Inhabitants* of *Light* and *Darkness*: *Come, you Blessed*, and *Go, you Cursed* <note>A reference to the Day of Judgment in Matthew 25:34 and 25:41.</note>. In a word then, *The Curse* was *Nothing* else but an *Act repealed*, or a *Restraint* of those *Blessings* which *God*, out of his pure *Goodness*, had formerly communicated to

Here we see the *first fruits* of the *spirit* applied to *man*. Why then should there not be some *secondary, subordinate fruits* of it for *creatures* in *general*? # The Antiquity of Magic

...mirth, whenever you give me such a good reason to laugh. It remains, then, a most firm and **infallible foundation** that **Adam** was first **instructed concerning the Passion** [of Christ], and in order for that to happen, he was **further taught to sacrifice** and offer up the **blood of beasts** as **types** <term>prophetic symbols or foreshadowing of a future reality</term> and **precursors** <note>original: "Prodroms," meaning forerunners or early signs of something to follow</note> of the **blood of Jesus Christ**. Now, if it is argued that various nations have practiced sacrifice who did not know God at all—much less the **Son of God**, who is the **prototype** and **perfection** of all **offerings** <note>original: "Oblations"</note>—to this I answer that the **custom of sacrificing** was passed down to the **heathens** by **tradition from the first man** [Adam]. Having instructed his own **children**, they in turn delivered it to their **posterity**. Thus, this **mask of religion** <note>original: "Vizard," meaning a mask or outward disguise</note> remained, even though the **substance** and **true doctrine** of it were lost. 

And so, in my **opinion**, it sufficiently appears that the first men did sacrifice not by **nature**—as **Porphyry** <note>original: "Prophyrius," a 3rd-century Neoplatonist philosopher who was a famous critic of Christianity.</note>, that **enemy of our religion**, would have it—but some by **revelation** and others by **custom and tradition**.

to tread on Ruines, *Cornelius Agrippa*, hath already layd these <term>Rodomontados|boastful and extravagant claims</term> in the *Dirt*, and that so handsomely, they were never since of a general Reputation. <margin><unclear>mostly</unclear> by searching the Brain</margin>
him; An *Art* that is no way subject to *Evill*, and by which I can attain to all the *Secrets* and *Mysteries in Nature*. This is the *Art* wherein the *Physics* of *Adam*, and the *Patriarchs* consisted, and that this Art was revealed to him, I will undertake to demonstrat by *Scriptures*, and the *Practice* of his *Posteritie*.

This Truth, I am certain, will seem difficult,

### Adamic Magic. <note>original title: "Magia Adamica"</note>

This is his mineral *Doctrine*; would you know his *Vegetable* <note>In 17th-century alchemy, the "vegetable" kingdom referred to the medicinal and spiritual properties of plants.</note>? Lastly, he has his *Animal Magic*, and truly that is a Scroll Sealed up; I know not who may open it. <margin>John chapter 2 verse 25.</margin> *He needed not that any should bear witness of man, for he knew what was in man.* And what of all this Blasphemy, says some *splenetic Sophister* <note>A "splenetic sophister" is a bitter or angry person who uses clever but misleading logical arguments.</note>?

Behold, I will instruct you. First of all, have Salt in yourself, for it will season your soul that is infested, and preserve your Brains that are putrefied with the Dirt of *Aristotle* <note>Vaughan, like many Hermetic philosophers, rejected the rigid logic of Aristotle taught in universities, believing it stifled true spiritual and scientific discovery.</note>. In the second place, learn what the Salt of the *Earth* is, to which the *Disciples* are compared, and that by a regular, solid speculation. Thirdly, come up to *Experience*, and by a *Physical* legitimate practice know in what sense *Salt* is *most good*. Fourthly, examine the *Lilies* by Fire, and the *Water of Fire* <note>Likely a reference to "Aqua Vitae" or a potent alchemical solvent used to extract the "essence" of a substance.</note>, that you may see their miraculous invisible Treasures, and wherein that speech of *Truth* is verified, *That Solomon in all his Royalty was not clothed like one of them.* 

If you will attempt a higher *Magic*, you may, being first seasoned; but in this place it is not my design to lead You to it. *Animal* and *Vegetable Mysteries* you can never perfectly obtain without the Knowledge of the first mineral secret, namely the Salt of the *Earth*, which is *Salt and no Salt* <note>An alchemical paradox referring to "philosophical salt," a substance that looks like salt but possesses unique, transformative properties.</note>, and the Pre-

### The Antiquity of Magic <note>original title: "The Antiquitie of Magic."</note>

...preparation thereof. I admit this discourse is somewhat far from my original intent—namely, that *Philosophy* was revealed to *Adam* just as *Divinity* was—but some *minds* <note>original: "Pates"</note> are *stubborn* <note>original: "Blocks," implying they are as unmoving and unthinking as blocks of wood</note> in their own ways. As I told you before, they refuse to believe that *God* shares any *Natural* secrets. This led me to produce these few *examples* from *Scripture* as *preliminary proofs* for the *proposition* itself, and for the *Reader*, provided he is at all clever. I do not expect him to agree with my *principles*; indeed, I am so far from expecting it that he may even withhold his *charity* <note>In this context, "charity" means a kind or favorable interpretation of the author's work.</note>. Let him be as rigid as *Justice* can make him, for I do not wish to *prevail* in anything but the *Truth*, and in the *Name* of *Truth*, I begin thus.

You have been told before that *Cain* and *Abel* were taught about *matters* of *Sacrifice* by their father *Adam*. But after *Cain* murdered his brother *Abel*, his *priesthood* passed down to *Seth*. This is confirmed by the *spiritual gifts* <note>original: "Faculties"</note> seen in his *descendants*, for *Enoch*, *Lamech*, and *Noah* were all *Prophets*. It might bother you that I attribute a *priesthood* to *Abel*, but in addition to his own actions, I have *Christ's* testimony for it. <margin>Luke chapter 11 verse 51 and Matthew 23:35.</margin> Christ counts the *blood* of *Abel* among that of the persecuted *prophets* and *wise men*. He weighed (says he) *Aleph* with *All*, and *All* with *Aleph*, and so he did with the other *Mothers*. This is very plain, if you consider the various mixtures of the *Elements*, and their *Secret Proportions*. And so much for the *Physical* part of the *Kabbalah*, I will now show you the *Metaphysical*. It is strange to *Consider* what *Unity* of *Spirit*, and *Doctrine*, there is amongst all the *Children* of *Wisdom*. This proves infallibly that there is a *Universal Schoolmaster*, who is *Present* with all *Flesh*, and whose *Principles* are ever *Uniform*, namely the *Spirit* of *God*. <meta>This page transitions from the specific alchemical imagery of Flamel’s figures to a broader philosophical defense of Magic as a divine science. # The Antiquitie of Magic.

I could now pass from *Moses* to *Christ*, from the *Old Testament* to the *New*: not that I would offer a standard interpretation there, but rather request the insights of the *Illuminated* <note>The "Illuminated" refers to those who possess spiritual or esoteric insight, such as the "magi" or "adepts" who look for deeper meanings in scripture.</note>. I desire to know what my *Savior* means by the *Key of Knowledge*, <margin>Luke chapter 11, verse 52</margin> which the *Lawyers* (as he tells me and them too) had taken away. Without question, it cannot signify the *Law* itself, for that was not taken away, being read in the *Synagogue* every *Sabbath*. But to let this go: I am certain, and I could prove it all along from his *Birth* to his *Passion*, that the *Doctrine* of *Christ Jesus* is not only agreeable to the *Laws* of *Nature*, but is verified and established by them. When I speak of the *Laws* of *Nature*, I do not mean her *excessive and irregular appetites* and *inclinations*, to which she has been subject since her *Corruption* <note>Referring to the "Fall of Adam," which was believed to have physically and spiritually corrupted the entire natural world.</note>; for even *Galen* <note>Claudius Galenus (129–c. 216 AD), the influential Greek physician whose theories on the four humors dominated medicine for over a millennium.</note> looked on those deviations as *diseases*, but studied *Nature herself* as their *cure*. We know by experience that too much of any thing weakens and destroys our Nature, but if we live *temperately* and according to *Law*, we are well, because our *course* of life accords with *Nature*. Hence *Diet* is a primary *Rule* in *Medicine* <term>Physic: The historical term for the practice and theory of medicine.</term>, far better indeed than the *Pharmacopoeia* <term>Pharmacopoeia: A standard collection of drug recipes; here used to disparage the complex and often toxic chemical concoctions of contemporary doctors.</term>, for those messy *prescriptions* <note>original: "recepts"</note> do nothing but burden the *stomach*, being no fit fuel for a *celestial fire* <note>In alchemical and mystical medicine, this is the "Archaeus" or vital life-force that sustains the body.</note>. Believe it then, these excessive, beast-like *appetites* proceeded from our *Fall*, for *Nature of her*

<meta>This page continues the argument from the previous section, shifting from a general discussion of temperance and natural law to the metaphysical harmony between the laws of the Gospel and the laws of Creation. # Magia Adamica, or

Nature itself is not some lavish, insatiable *hunger* <note>original: "Glut," referring here to an overindulgent or uncontrollable appetite.</note>, but a most *refined and delicate essence*. This is proven by those fits and pangs she is *subject* to whenever she is overcharged. In common, habitual excesses, there is no one who does not know this *truth* by *experience*; indeed, in *spiritual sins*, the *body* is not immediately troubled, but the *conscience* is terrified, and surely the body cannot be very *well* when the *soul itself* is sick. 

We see then that *corruption and sin* do not so much agree with us as they do disturb us; for in what sense can our *enemies* be our friends, or those things that destroy *nature* be agreeable to *nature*? How then shall we judge the *Gospel*? Shall we say that the *preservation of Man* is contrary to *Man*, and that the *Doctrine of Life* does not agree with *life itself*? 

God forbid: The Laws of the Resurrection are founded upon those of the Creation, and those of Regeneration upon those of Generation, for in all these, God works upon one and the same matter by one and the same spirit. 

Now, to show that this is so—I mean that there is a *harmony* between *nature* and the *gospel*—I will prove it using the *Chinese Monument* <note>original: "Sinic Monument." This refers to the Nestorian Stele, a famous artifact discovered in 1625 that recorded the arrival of Christianity in China during the Tang Dynasty.</note> of *Kim Cim*, a priest of *Judea*. In the year of Redemption 1625, there was dug up in a village of *China* called *Sanxuen* <note>Likely referring to Xi'an or the district of Zhouzhi where the stele was found.</note>, a square stone, being nearly ten hand-breadths long,

# Magia Adamica, or

then *Philosophers*. Lastly he describes the professors of *Christianitie*, with their *Habit of Life*, and the *Excellencie* of their *Law*. It is difficult (he says) to find a suitable name for it, since its effect is to illuminate and bathe all things in brightness; hence it was necessary to call it; *Kim ki ao*, that is, the Great and Clear Law. <note>original: "Difficile (saith he) est ei Nomen Congruum reperire, cum ejus effectus sit Illuminare, & omnia Claritate perfundere; unde Necessarium fuit eam appellare; Kim ki ao, h. e. Legem claram & magnam." The term "Kim ki ao" refers to Jingjiao, the "Luminous Religion," which was the name for Nestorian Christianity in Tang Dynasty China.</note> That is : It is a hard matter to find a fit name for their Law, seeing the effect of it is to illuminate, and fill all with Knowledge ; It was necessary therefore to call it *Kim ki ao*, that is, the great *Law of Light*.

To be short, *Olopuen* <note>Alopen, the first recorded Christian missionary to reach China, arriving in 635 AD.</note> was admitted to the Court by *Tai cum veu huamti* <note>Emperor Taizong, the second emperor of the Tang Dynasty, known for his religious tolerance.</note> King of *China*, here his *Doctrine* was thoroughly searched, examin’d, and sifted by the King himself, who having found it most true and solid, caus’d it to be proclaim’d thorough his *Dominions*. Now upon what this *Doctrine* was founded, and what *estimat* the *King* had both of it, and its *professor*, we may easily gather from the words of his Proclamation. First then, where he mentions *Olopuen*, he calls him a Man of Great Virtue <note>original: "Magnæ virtutis Hominem"</note> or power ; it seems he did something more than prate and preach, could confirme his *Doctrine*, as the *Apostles* did theirs, not with words only, but with works. Secondly the Proclamation speaking of his *Doctrine*,

### The Antiquity of Magic.

...runs thus: *Whose intent in teaching we have examined from the very foundations, and we have found his doctrine to be exceedingly excellent and without outward noise, founded principally on the creation of the world.* <note>original: "Cujus intentum docendi nos a Fundamentis examinantes, invenimus Doctrinam ejus admodum excellentem, & fine strepitu exteriori, fundatam principaliter in Creatione Mundi"</note> That is, the drift of whose teaching we have examined from the very fundamentals, we find his doctrine very excellent, without any worldly noise, and principally grounded on the creation of the world. And again in the same place: *His doctrine is not of many words, nor does he establish his truth only on the surface.* <note>original: "Doctrina ejus non est multorum verborum, nec superficie tenus suam fundat Veritatem"</note> His doctrine is but of few words, not full of noise and notions, neither does he build his truth on superficial probabilities.

Thus we see the *Incarnation* and *Birth of Christ Jesus*—which to the common philosopher are fables and impossibilities, but in the **Book of Nature** are plain, evident truths—were proved and demonstrated by the early Apostles and teachers out of the **Creation of the World**. <note>The "Book of Nature" is a theological concept suggesting that God is revealed through the natural world just as he is through scripture.</note>

But instead of such teachers, we have in these our days two widespread "goblins": a **Schoolman** <note>A follower of Scholasticism, a method of learning common in medieval universities that focused heavily on Aristotelian logic and rigid dialectical reasoning.</note> and a **"Saint,"** forsooth. The one swells with a *syllogistic* pride <note>A syllogism is a form of logical argument (e.g., All men are mortal; Socrates is a man; therefore Socrates is mortal). Vaughan is criticizing those who rely solely on logic rather than physical or spiritual reality.</note>, the other wears a broad face of *Revelation*. The first cannot tell me why *grass is green*; the second, with all his *devotion*, knows not his *ABC's*, yet he pretends to possess that **Infinite Spirit** which knows all in all; and truly...

# Magia Adamica, or

of them both, this last is the worst. Surely the **Devil** has been very busy to put out the **Candle**; <note>A metaphor for the light of divine truth or human reason.</note> for had all written **Truths** survived, this false learning and **Hypocrisy** could never have prevailed. **Kim Cim** <note>Likely a reference to the Chinese text or the "Illustrious Religion" (Jingjiao) described on the Nestorian Stele mentioned on the previous page.</note> mentions twenty-seven books which **Jesus Christ** left on Earth to further the **Conversion** of the **world**. It may be that we possess not a single one of them: for though the books of the New Testament are exactly twenty-seven in number, yet because they were all written—or at least some of them—a long time after **Christ**, they cannot properly be considered those **Scriptures** which this **Author** attributes to our **Saviour**, even at the time of his **Ascension**. Why should I speak of those many books cited in the Old Testament that are nowhere to be found? If they were extant today, there is no doubt they would prove to be venerable and invincible defenders of **Magic**. But **ink** and **paper** will **perish**, for the hand of **Man** has made nothing eternal. Only the **Truth** is **incorruptible**; and when the **written word** fails, she sheds that **body** and **lives** in the **spirit**.

I have not without significant effort now traced this **Science** from the very **Fall of Man** to the **Day of his Redemption**. It has been a long and lonely pilgrimage; the paths are seldom traveled because of the thorns and the difficult questions of **Antiquity**, and in some places, they are overgrown with the **Poppy** of **Obli-** <note>The catchword "vion" on the next page completes the word "Oblivion," symbolizing the forgetfulness or loss of historical memory.</note>

The next stage I must move to is that whence I came out at first with the *Israelites*, namely *Egypt*; here if *books* fail me, the stones will cry out; *Magic* having been so enthroned in this place, it seems she would be buried here also: so many *monuments* did she hide in this earth, which have been since digged up; and serve now to prove, that she was sometimes above ground. To begin then, I will first speak of the *Egyptian theology*, that you may see how far they have advanced, having no *leader*, but the *Light of Nature* <term>Light of Nature: The inherent wisdom found in the physical world and human reason, independent of revealed scripture.</term>. *Trismegistus* <note>Hermes Trismegistus, the legendary figure to whom the Hermetic writings are attributed; the author views him as a pagan prophet who anticipated Christian truths.</note> is so orthodox and plain in the *mystery* of the *Trinity*, the Scripture itself exceeds him not; but he being a particular *author*, and one perhaps that knew more than those of his order in general, I shall at this time dispense with his *authority*. Their *universal doctrine* <note>Original: "Catholic Doctrine." The author uses "Catholic" in its original sense of "universal" or "general," rather than referring to the Roman Catholic Church.</note>, and wherein I find them all to agree, is *this*.

# Magia Adamica, or <note>The full title of the work is "Magia Adamica; or, The Antiquitie of Magic."</note>

Emepht <note>A name used in Hermetic literature (likely derived from the Egyptian 'Kneph' or 'Amun') to describe the unmanifested creator God.</note>, whereby they express their Supreme God, and verily they mind the true One; signifies properly an *Intelligence*, or *Spirit* converting all things into himself, and himself into all Things. This is very sound *Divinity* and *Philosophy*, if it be rightly understood. Now (say they) *Emepht* produced an *Egg* out of his *mouth*, which Tradition *Kircher* <note>Athanasius Kircher (1602–1680), a famous Jesuit scholar and polymath who wrote extensively—though often inaccurately—on Egyptian hieroglyphs.</note> expounds *imperfectly*, and withal *Erroneously*. In the *Production* of this *Egg* was manifested another *Deity*, which they call *Phtha* <note>The Egyptian god Ptah, the demiurge or divine craftsman who forms the physical world.</note>, and out of some other *Natures* and *Substances* enclosed in the *Egg*, this *Phtha* formed *all Things*. But to deal a little more openly, we will describe unto you their *Hieroglyphic* <term>Hieroglyphic: In the 17th century, these were viewed not just as a writing system, but as sacred, mathematical symbols containing the deep secrets of nature.</term>, wherein they have very handsomely, but obscurely discovered most of their *Mysteries*. First of all then, they draw a *Circle*, in the *Circle* a *Serpent*, not *folded*, but *Diameter-wise*, and at *length*; her *head* resembles that of a *Hawk*, the *Tail* is tied in a *Small Knot*, and a little below the head her wings are *Volant* <note>Volant: A heraldic term meaning "in the act of flying" or with wings spread wide.</note>. The *Circle* points at *Emepht*, or God the *Father* being *Infinite*, without *Beginning*, without *End*. Moreover it comprehends, or *Contains* in itself the Second *Deity Phtha*, and the *Egg*, or *Chaos* <note>In this context, Chaos refers to the "First Matter," the unformed substance from which the universe was organized.</note>, out of which *all Things* were made. The *Hawk* in the *Egyptian Symbols* signifies *Light*, and *Spirit*; his head annexed here to the *Serpent*

In the second place, they ought to know there is a *Universal Patient* <term>Universal Patient: The passive, receptive substance of the universe that receives the "form" or "ideas" from the Agent; the raw material of creation</term>, and this *Passive Nature* was created by the *Universal Agent*. This general Patient is the immediate *Universal Character* <note>original: "Catholic Character"; here "catholic" is used in its original sense meaning "universal" or "all-encompassing"</note> of God himself in his *Unity* and *Trinity*. In plain *Terms*, it is that *Substance* which we commonly call the *first Matter* <term>first Matter: (Latin: materia prima) The original, chaotic substance from which all physical things were created, sought by alchemists as the starting point for the Philosopher’s Stone</term>. 

But truly, it is to no purpose to know this concept of *Matter* unless we know the *Thing* itself to which the *Concept* relates; we must see it, handle it, and by experimental, visual demonstrations know the very central, *Invisible Essences* and *Properties* of it. # Heaven of the Earth, or the <note>original title: "Cœlum Terræ"</note>

...alterable. <note>original Latin: "alterabilem"; this word completes the sentence from the previous page</note> *For let pardon be granted (as Madaurensis <term>Madaurensis: Refers to Apuleius of Madaura, a 2nd-century North African philosopher and author of "The Golden Ass"</term> says) to the Novelty of Words <note>original: "Novitati Verborum"</note>, which serves the obscurities of things. This very thing, since it cannot persist as the same and one, is nonetheless received by the hospitality of such a power of the mind, in a manner more rightly according to what it is, than what it is not, as the thing is in truth, that is, changeable. However, the other nature of substances is incorruptible, immutable, constant, and the same and always existing.*

The English of it speaks thus. One Nature is such, it may be seen with the eyes, and felt with the hands, and it is subject to *Alteration* almost in every Moment. You must Pardon (as *Apuleius* saith) this strange Expression, because it makes for the *Obscurity* of the *Thing*. This very Nature, since she may not continue one, and the same, is notwithstanding apprehended of the mind under her such Qualification, more rightly as she is, than as she is not, namely as the Thing itself is in *Truth*, that is to say, Changeable. The other Nature, or Principle of Substances, is incorruptible, immutable, constant, One and the same for ever, and always existent. Thus he. Now this Changeable Nature whereof he speaks, is the *first Visible, Tangible Substance* that ever *God* made; it is white in Appearance, and *Paracelsus* <term>Paracelsus: A 16th-century Swiss physician and alchemist who pioneered the use of chemicals and minerals in medicine</term> gives you the Reason why: *All things (says he) in God's Hand are white, and he tints them as he pleases.* <note>original Latin: "Omnia in Dei Manu alba sunt, is ea tingit, ut vult"</note> All

# Cœlum Terræ, or the
### Magician's Heavenly Chaos. 84

She yields to nothing but *Love*, for her end is *Generation* <term>Generation: The process of bringing forth life or new form in matter.</term>, and *that* was never yet performed by *Violence*. He that knows how to wanton and *toy* with her, the same shall receive all her *Treasures*. First, she sheds at her *Nipples* a thick heavy water, but white as any snow; the Philosophers call it *Virgin-milk* <note>A symbolic term for the first pure liquid state of the alchemical matter, often associated with the 'mercurial' stage.</note>. Secondly, she gives him *Blood* from her very heart; it is a quick heavenly fire, some improperly call it their *sulphur* <term>Sulphur: In alchemy, this represents the active, masculine, and combustible principle within a substance.</term>. Thirdly and lastly, she presents him with a secret *Crystal*, of more worth and luster than the *white Rock*, and all her *Rosials* <note>Likely referring to "Rosy" or reddish substances produced during the final stages of the Great Work.</note>. This is she, and these are her *Favors*: Catch her, if you can.

Lastly, they say she is a middle nature between thick and thin, neither altogether Earthy, nor altogether Fiery, but a middle aerial substance to be found everywhere, and at every time of the year.

The *earth* swallows everything and, like that *philosophical dragon* <note>The Ouroboros—the ancient symbol of a snake or dragon eating its own tail—representing the cyclical nature of life, death, and the alchemical process where matter is constantly recycled.</note>, eats her own *tail*. # The Heaven of the Earth <note>original: "Cælum Terræ"</note>, or the, etc.

*Have you beheld a Flame, that springs*

*Of smoke attend her last, weak Fires,*
*And She all in Perfumes expires?*

*And into Waters all distilled.*
*It is constant still: Trust not false Smiles,*
*Who smiles, and weeps not, she beguiles.*
*Nay, trust not Tears: false are the few,*
*Those Tears are Many, that are True.*
*Trust Me, and take the better Choice,*
*Who hath my Tears, can want no Joys.*

...the flights of my *Verse*, or follow the more grave pace of their *Prose*. This is the *Book of Three Words*, meaning thereby *Three Principles*, the Book of the Precious stone, which is a Body aerial and volatile, cold and moist, watery and parched, and in it is Heat and Drought, Coldness and Moisture, one virtue inwardly, the other outwardly. *Belus* the Philosopher, in that famous and most classic assembly of *Arisleus*, <note>The "Synod of Arisleus" refers to the *Turba Philosophorum*, one of the earliest and most influential Latin alchemical texts, presented as a debate among ancient philosophers.</note> inverts the order to conceal the **practice**, but if rightly understood, he speaks to the purpose. <meta>This page continues the discourse on the dual nature of the "First Matter," moving from the physical descriptions of its temperature and moisture to its fundamental identity as "Permanent Water." -># Cælum Terræ, or the<-
->### Magician's Heavenly Chaos###<-

...that it is a stone is, among the unlearned, <term>Idiotas: Referring to the "unskilled" or those not initiated into the alchemical arts, rather than a modern insult.</term> worthless and incredible. For who will believe that Stone becomes Water, and Water becomes Stone, when nothing is more different? Yet in truth it is so. For the Stone is this very permanent water, <term>Permanent water: Also known as "aqua permanens," a central alchemical concept referring to a universal solvent that does not evaporate when heated and can dissolve metals into their primary essence.</term> and while it is water, it is not a stone. <note>original Latin: "esse lapidem, apud Idiotas vile & Incredibile. Quis enim credet Lapidem Aquam, & Aquam Lapidem fieri, cum nihil sit diversius? Attamen revera ita est. Lapis enim est hæc ipsa per manens Aqua, & dum Aqua est lapis non est."</note> Amongst all great Philosophers it is Magisterial <note>Magisterial: In this context, it means a fundamental or "master" principle that is accepted as authoritative truth.</note> that our stone is no stone, but amongst Ignorants it is ridiculous and incredible. For who will believe that water can be made a stone, and a stone water, nothing being more different than these two? And yet in very truth it is so. For this very permanent water is the stone, but whiles it is water, it is no stone.

But in this sense the Ancient *Hermes* <note>Hermes Trismegistus: The legendary Hellenistic figure to whom the foundation of alchemy (Hermeticism) is attributed.</note> abounds, and almost discovers too much. "Know, O Sons of the Wise, that the water of the ancient Philosophers is a Division, which divides itself into four other things." <note>original Latin: "Scitote Filii Sapientum, quod priscorum Philosophorum aqua est Divisio, quæ dividit ipsam in Alia quatuor."</note> Know (saith hee) you that are the Children of the wise, the Separation of the ancient Philosophers was performed upon water, which Separation divides the water into other foure Substances. <note>The four substances refers to the four elements: Earth, Air, Fire, and Water, which alchemists believed could be extracted from the First Matter.</note>

There is extant a very learned Author, who hath written something to this purpose, and that more openly than any, whom we have formerly cited. "Just as the World owes its origin to the Water, upon which the Spirit of the Lord brooded, <note>A reference to the Book of Genesis, where the Spirit of God moves over the face of the waters at the moment of creation.</note> with all things both Heavenly and Earthly proceeding from there; so this Limbo <term>Limbus: A term for the "great border" or the primordial matter from which all things are organized; it is the chaotic "stuff" of the universe before it takes specific form.</term> emerges from water that is not common, nor from Heavenly Dew, nor from the air—" <note>original Latin: "Sicuti Mundus Originem debet Aquæ, cui Spiritus Domini incubabat, rebus tam Cælestibus, quàm Terrestribus omnibus indè prodeuntibus; ita Limbus hic emergit ex Aqua non vulgari, neq; ex Rore Cælesti, aut ex aere"</note>

<meta>This page continues the argument that the "First Matter" or "Chaos" of the alchemists is not found in common bodies of water but in a specific, "tortured" substance. It transitions from general philosophical claims to a specific citation from a Rosicrucian source.</meta>

### Magician's Heavenly Chaos ###

*...condensed in the Caverns of the Earth, or in the **Receiver** itself; not drawn from the Abyss of the Sea, from springs, wells, or rivers, but from a certain **tortured water** <term>Tortured water: From the Latin "aqua perpessâ," this refers to a substance that has undergone "suffering" or chemical alteration—usually through heat or fermentation—to change its state from common to philosophical.</term>, obvious to all, but known to very few, which has within itself whatever is necessary for the completion of the whole work, all extrinsic things having been removed.* <note>original Latin: "Condensato in Cavernis Terræ, vel in Recipiente ipso, non ex Abysso Maris, fontibus, puteis, fluminibusve hausto, sed ex Aquâ quadam perpessâ, omnibus obviâ, paucissimis cognitâ, Quæ in se habet, quæcunq; ad totius operis Complementum sunt necessaria, omni amoto Extrinseco."</note>

As the world (saith he) was generated out of that *Water*, upon which the *Spirit of God* did move, all things proceeding thence, both *Celestial* and *Terrestrial*, so this *Chaos* is generated out of a certain *Water* that is not common, not out of *Dew*, nor *Air* condensed in the *Caverns* of the *Earth*, or Artificially in the *Receiver*; not out of water drawn out of the *Sea*, *Fountains*, *Wells*, or *Rivers*, but out of a certain *tortured water*, that hath suffered some *Alteration*, obvious it is to *All*, but known to very few. This *water* hath all in it, that is necessary to the perfection of the work, without any *Extrinsic Addition*. I could produce a Thousand Authors more, but that were tedious; I shall conclude with one of the *Rosie Brothers* <term>Rosie Brothers: The Rosicrucians, a legendary and secretive brotherhood of mystics and alchemists who claimed to possess the secrets of the "Universal Reformation" and the Philosopher's Stone.</term>, whose Testimony is *Equivalent* to the Best of These, but his Instruction far more Excellent. His Discourse of the *first Matter* <term>First Matter: Often called "materia prima," this is the original, formless substance from which all things were created and which alchemists sought to isolate as the starting point for their Great Work.</term> is somewhat large, and to avoid prolixity, I shall forbear the *Latin*, but I will give thee his Sense in punctual plain *English*.

O most excellent *Subject*! out of which all things in this world are generated, though at the first sight thou art *Poison*, adorned with the name of the flying *Eagle* <term>Eagle: An alchemical symbol for volatile substances that "fly" or rise during distillation.</term>. Thy Parents are the *Sun* and *Moon*; in Thee there is *Water* and *Wine*, *Gold* also and *Silver* upon *Earth*, that mortal man may rejoice. After this manner God sends us his *Blessing* and *Wisdom* with *Rain*, and the *Beams* of the *Sun*, to the eternal Glory of his *Name*. ### Magicians Heavenly Chaos.

Property, and Nature of a **Salamander** <term>Salamander: In alchemy, a mythical lizard-like creature that lives in fire; it represents the "Fixed" or non-combustible substance that survives the furnace.</term>, which, if it remains in the fire without any detriment, it cures all the Diseases of Men, Beasts, and Metals. After that the **Ancient Philosophers** had perfectly understood this Subject, they diligently sought in this Mystery for the Center of the **Middlemost Tree** in the **Terrestrial Paradise** <note>Terrestrial Paradise: The Garden of Eden, often used by alchemists to describe the original, pure state of matter.</note>, entering in by **Five contentious Gates** <term>Contentious: The original text uses "litigious," meaning these stages are full of difficulty and "conflict" between opposing elements.</term>. 

The **first Gate** was the Knowledge of the true Matter, and here arose the first, and that a most bitter Conflict. The **second** was the Preparation by which this matter was to be prepared, that they might obtain the **Embers of the Eagle**, and the **Blood of the Lion** <term>Eagle and Lion: The Eagle represents the volatile (evaporating) spirit, and the Lion represents the fixed (solid) body. Combining them is a central goal of alchemy.</term>. At this Gate there is a most sharp fight, for it produces water and blood, and a **Spiritual bright Body**. The **Third Gate** is the **Fire**, which leads to the **Maturity** of the Medicine. The **Fourth Gate** is that of **Multiplication** and **Augmentation** <term>Multiplication: The process of increasing the power and quantity of the alchemical medicine.</term> in which Proportions and Weights are Necessary. The **fifth** and last Gate is **Projection** <term>Projection: The final stage where the Philosopher's Stone is cast onto base metals to transform them into gold.</term>. 

But most glorious, full rich, and high is he who attains to the fourth Gate, for he has got a **Universal Medicine** for all Diseases. This is that great Character of the **Book of Nature**, out of which her whole Alphabet does arise.

# The Heaven of the Earth <note>original Latin: "Cœlum Terræ"</note>, or the

The fifth gate serves only for **Metals**. This Mystery, existing from the Foundation of the World and the Creation of **Adam**, is of all others the most ancient; it is a knowledge which God Almighty, by His Word, breathed into Nature as a miraculous power—the blessed fire of Life, the **Transparent Carbuncle** <term>Carbuncle: Historically a deep red gemstone, but in alchemy, it refers to the glowing, perfected state of the Philosopher's Stone.</term>, and the **red Gold** of the Wise men, and the Divine Benediction of this life. But this mystery, because of the malice and wickedness of men, is given only to few; notwithstanding, it lives and moves every day in the sight of the whole world, as it appears by the following parable:

> *I am a poisonous **Dragon**, present everywhere, and to be had for nothing. My water and my fire dissolve and compound; out of my body thou shalt draw the **Green Lion**, and the **Red Lion** <term>Green and Red Lion: Symbols for the stages of the alchemical process; the Green Lion is often the raw mineral acid or "solvent," while the Red Lion is the stabilized, "fixed" matter.</term>. # Cælum Terræ, or the

...the **Carbuncle of the Sun** <term>Carbuncle of the Sun: A symbolic name for the Philosopher's Stone in its red, perfected state, likened to a glowing, precious gemstone.</term>, a most noble clarified **Earth**, by which you may turn **Copper, Iron, Tin**, and **Lead** into most pure **Gold**. <insert>and your five senses into <unclear>piety</unclear>.</insert>

Now, gentlemen, you may see the direction in which the **Philosophers** move: they praise their **Secret Water**, and I admire the **Tears of Hyanthe** <note>Hyanthe is the author’s poetic name for the "First Matter"—the divine, watery substance from which the world was created.</note>. There is something in this **imagination** besides mere **poetry**, for my **Mistress** is very **philosophical**, and in her **love** she is a pure **Platonic** <note>The author is suggesting his "Mistress"—the alchemical substance—is a spiritual entity that requires a refined, intellectual love rather than a physical one.</note>. But now that I think of it, how many 
<margin>Very few, I dare swear.</margin> 
**rivals** shall **I** create for myself by this **discourse**? Every reader will try to win her, and some "fine fellow" may break her heart with **nonsense**. This **love** would indeed be pure **luck**; but for my part, I dare trust her. And lest any man should mistake her for any of the substances formerly named, I will tell you truly what she is: 

She is not any **known water** whatsoever, but a **secret, generative moisture**, or rather the **Venus** <term>Venus: In alchemy, this represents the feminine, receptive principle that provides the "seed" or life-force necessary for growth and transformation.</term> that yields that **moisture**. Therefore, do not imagine that she is any **raw, phlegmatic, thin water**, for she is a **fat, thick, heavy, slimy humidity**. But lest you should think I have grown jealous and would not trust you with my **Mistress**, **Arnaldus de Villa Nova** <note>A famous 13th-century physician and alchemist whose works were highly influential.</note> shall speak for me; hear him: **Further—** <note>original: "Am-" (catchword for "Amplius"), meaning "furthermore" or "moreover."</note>

### Magician’s Heavenly Chaos

*I tell you further, that we could in no way find, nor in like manner could the Philosophers find, any thing persisting in the fire, except only the oily moisture. We see the watery moisture easily evaporate; the dry parts remain, and they are therefore separated because they are not natural. But if we were to consider those moistures which are with difficulty separated from those parts which are natural to them, we find none except the oily and sticky ones.* <note>original: "plius tibi dico... unctuosas, & viscosas." In alchemy, this refers to a stable, oily substance that acts as the "glue" of matter, capable of enduring high heat without vanishing.</term>. A watery **Humidity**, we see, will easily vapor away, and the **Earth** remains behind, and the parts are therefore separated, because their **Composition** is not natural. But if we consider those humidities, which are hardly separated from those parts which are natural to them, we find not any such, but the ***unctuous, viscous Humidities***. ### Magician's Heavenly Chaos.

*It is nothing of those things that are, and nothing of those things that are not.* <note>original Latin: "Nihil eorum quæ sunt, & Nihil eorum quæ non sunt."</note> It is nothing that was created, or of those things that exist: and nothing of that which you call "nothing"—that is, of those things that do not exist—in your empty, destructive sense. Or was the *world*, and *all* that is therein, made of *man’s Blood*, as of their *First Matter*? <term>First Matter</term> <note>Also called "Prima Materia"; the fundamental, formless substance from which God created all physical things.</note> Surely no such Thing. Take heed then; let not any man deceive thee. But I had almost forgotten to tell thee that, which is all in all, and it is the greatest Difficulty in all the *Art*, namely the fire. It is a close, airy, circular, bright fire; the Philosophers call it their *Sun*, and the glass must stand in the shade. It makes not the matter to vapor, no, not so much as to sweat; it digests only with a still, piercing, vital heat. It is continual, and therefore at last alters the *Chaos* <note>The "Chaos" refers to the prime, unrefined matter at the start of the alchemical work.</note>, and corrupts it; The *Proportion* and *Regiment* <note>The management or regulation of the heat.</note> of it is very *Scrupulous*, but the best rule to know it by, is that of the *Synod*: *make it so that the Pheasant does not fly before the hunter* <note>original: "facite nè Phasianus volet ante Insequentem." If the heat is too high, the spirit escapes (flies away) before the alchemist (the hunter) can capture and fix it.</note>; Let not the *Bird* fly before the *Fowler*; make it sit while you give fire, and then you are sure of

your prey. To conclude, I must tell you, the philosophers called this *fire* their *bath*, but it is a *Bath of Nature* <note>original: "Balneum Naturæ"</note>, a natural bath, not an *artificial* one; for it is not any kind of *water*, but a certain *subtle, temperate moisture* which surrounds the glass and feeds their *sun*, or *fire*. In a word, without this *bath* nothing in the world is generated. Now, so that you may better understand what *degree of fire* is required for the work, consider the *generation* of *man*, or any other creature whatsoever. It is not *kitchen fire*, nor a fever that works upon the *sperm* in the womb, but a most *temperate, moist, natural heat*, which proceeds from the *very life* of the *mother*. It is just so here; our *matter* is a most *delicate substance*, and tender like the *animal sperm*, for it is almost a *living thing*—nay, in very truth it has some small portion of life, for *Nature* does produce some *animals* out of it. For this very reason, the least violence destroys it and prevents all *generation*; for if it is overheated even for a few minutes, the *white* and *red sulphurs* <note>In alchemy, the "white" and "red" sulphurs refer to the purified active principles of the matter at different stages of its development toward the Philosopher's Stone.</note> will never *essentially unite* and *coagulate*. On the contrary, if it takes cold for even half an hour—the work being once well begun—it will never result in any good purpose. I speak from my own experience, for I have (as the saying goes) given myself a box on the ear <note>A phrase meaning to learn a painful lesson through one’s own errors.</note>, and that twice

# Cœlum Terræ, or the

Subject they found the Natures of all particulars, and this is signified to us by that Maxim: *He who knows not Proteus, let him go to Pan.* <note>original Latin: "Qui Proteum non novit, adeat Pana." Now to reduce *Bodies* into *Elements* of *earth* and *water*, as we have demonstrated in the *Egg*, is to reduce them into *Extremes* beyond their *Sperm*; for *Elements* are not the *Sperm*, but the *Sperm* is a *Compound* made of the *Elements*, and containing in itself *all* that is required to the frame of the *Body*. Wherefore be well advised before you *distill*, and *quarter* any particular *Bodies*, for having once separated their *Elements*, you may never *generate*, unless you can make a *Sperm* of those *Elements*—but that is impossible for man to do, it is the Power of *God*, and *Nature*. Labor then you that would be accounted wise, to find out our *Mercury*, so shall you reduce things to their middle *spermatic Chaos*, but avoid the broiling *Destruction*. ### Heaven of Earth, or the

->I 2<-

...of Truth: That *Sperms* <note>In alchemy, "Sperm" refers to the "First Matter" or the generative essence of a substance, rather than biological semen.</note> are not made by *Separation*, but by *Composition* of *Elements*, and to bring a Body into *Sperm*, is not to *distill* it, but to reduce the whole into one thick *water*, keeping all the parts thereof in their *first natural union*. But that I may return at last to my former Citation of the Synod <note>The "Synod" likely refers to the *Turba Philosophorum* (Assembly of the Philosophers), a famous early alchemical text where various sages debate the secrets of nature.</note>. All those *Influences* of the *Elements* being united in one *Mass* make our *Sperm*, or our *Earth*, which is *Earth* and *no Earth* <note>A classic alchemical paradox. It looks like common soil or solid matter but possesses spiritual and transformative properties that "common" earth lacks.</note>. Take it if thou doest know it and divide the *Essences* thereof, not by *violence* but by *natural putrefaction*, such as may occasion a *genuine Dissolution* of the *Compound*. 

Here thou shalt find a miraculous *white Water*, an Influence of the *Moon*, which is the *Mother* of our *Chaos*; It rules in two Elements, *Earth* and *Water*. After this appears the *Sperm* or influence of the *Sun*, which is the *father* of it. It is a quick <note>"Quick" here means "living" or "animated."</note>, *Celestial fire*, incorporated in a thin oily, *Aerial Moisture*. It is *incombustible*, for it is *fire* itself, and feeds upon *fire*, and the *longer* it stays in the *fire*, the more glorious it grows. These are the two mineral *Sperms*, *Masculine* and *Feminine*: if thou doest place them both on their *Crystalline Basis*, thou hast the Philosopher's *flying Fire-drake* <note>The "Fire-drake" or Dragon is a symbol for the volatile spirit. When the masculine (Sun/Sulfur) and feminine (Moon/Mercury) principles are united, they create a powerful, "poisonous" (highly reactive) substance that must be stabilized.</note>, which at the first sight of the *Sun* breathes such a poison, that nothing can stand before him. I know not what to tell thee more, unless in the *Vogue* <note>The text cuts off here; "vogue" likely refers to the common or popular understanding of these terms.</note>

But come what will come, I have once more spoken for the *Truth*, and shall for Conclusion speak this much Again. I have elsewhere call’d this *Subject*, *Heavenly Mud* <note>original Latin: *Limus cœlestis*. This term refers to the primordial, fertile substance from which the world was created—a mix of spirit and matter.</note>, and the *middle Nature*: The *Philosophers* call it the *Venerable Nature*, but amongst all the *Pretenders* I have not yet found one, that could tell me *why*. Hear me then, that whensoever thou doest attempt this work, it may be with reverence, not like some proud, ignorant *Doctor* but with less *Confidence* & more *Care*. This *Chaos* <note>In alchemy, 'Chaos' is not disorder, but the 'First Matter'—the unformed raw material containing all the elements necessary for the Great Work.</note> hath in it the four *Elements*, which of themselves are *contrary Natures*, but the wisdom of God hath so placed them, that their very order reconciles them. For Example, *Air* and *Earth* are *Adversaries*, for one is *hot* and *moist*, the other *cold* and *dry*. Now to reconcile these *two*, God placed the *Water* between them, which is a *middle Nature*, or of a mean *Complexion* <note>'Complexion' here refers to the specific combination of qualities (hot, cold, moist, dry) that defines a substance.</note> between both *Extremes*. For she is *cold* and *moist*, and as she is *cold*, she partakes of the *Nature* of the *Earth*, which is *cold* and *dry*, but as she is *moist*, she partakes in the *Nature* of the *Air*, which is *hot* and *moist*. Hence it is, that *Air* and *Earth* which are *Contraries*

This blessed *Spirit* fortifies, and perfects that weak *Disposition* which the *Elements* already have to *Union* and *Peace*, (for *God* works with *Nature*, not against her,) and brings them at last to a beauteous specific *Fabric* <note>meaning a completed, physical structure or body.</note>. To this I answer, that she is, as all *Instrumentals* <note>Tools or instruments used to achieve an end.</note> ought to be, subject and obedient to the will of *God*, expecting the perfection of her *Body*: for it is *God* that unites her to the *Body*, and the body to her. *Soul* and *Body* are the work of *God*, the one as well as the other: the *Soul* is not the *Artificer* <note>the craftsman or builder.</note> of her *house*, for that which can make a *Body*, can also *repair* it, and hinder death; but the *Soul* cannot do *this*, it is the *Power*, and *Wisdom* of *God*.
